## UX-04: primary exercise response and action cadence — 2026-10-23

**Baseline merged GitHub main:** UX-03 [PR #113](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/113) squash `17392c6c0874e6bbad3c524921937f6290d31df7`; four final-head checks green in [Actions #39927557018](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39927557018). Continuity checkpoint main `d462ee79b3f28f356cf26d2675527ccbded522d2`. Physical/manual device and native-speaker tests remain deferred.

**UX-04 independent branch `feat/v2-ux04-exercise-action-cadence` IN DEVELOPMENT until certified and merged:** learner-driven Practice/Read/Listen/Speak is the only feedback journey. When the learner chooses an objective Listen/Read answer, expose immediate two-state **Correct! / Not quite**; show source Polish and English without a separate diagnostic/advice paragraph or verbose transcript labels; leave one obvious `Continue →` that records the existing answer exactly once. Unassessed reveal uses consistent direct `Needs practice`/`Got it →` self-rating on Learn, Listen and Read. Preserve review scheduling, verified comprehension evidence, fixed choices, stop-audio on answer, double-activation locks, duration/timeout safety and all XP arithmetic.

**Progress and speech:** progress position is already in the lesson heading/meter, so remove the redundant `N of X explored` footer text but **retain accessible Previous/Skip**. Session completion retains the real item count, exact session XP (or explicit `No XP earned this session`), optional subsequent lesson and mode handoffs, 1–10 / 11–20 priority and no forced lesson advancement. Speak retains record/stop, replay, single-word text colour hints, real optional guide, optional explicit per-take upload, score, microphone errors and clear recovery; a ready recording shows `Recording ready` without the irrelevant duration, and the audio control is accessible without another visible label. Never auto-call providers or treat skipped/unscored takes as pronunciation grades.

**Visual treatment:** reuse existing vector sounds glyph instead of text-music icon, tighten result/next-action spacing and replace status-colour banners with direct readable text (not per-syllable boxes or added advice). No content rewrite, audio corpus changes, storage/schema modifications, personal/family XP changes or new gates.

**Evidence gate:** existing and new desktop+mobile browser journeys (objective Listen/Read, lesson footer, completion, mock microphone, connected family/consent/assessment) and all four final PR HEAD CI jobs must PASS before merge; track head SHA/actions run and merge SHA in this workflow and GitHub issue #2. Only synthetic accessibility/browser testing; physical Safari, realistic mic/audio and native linguistic validation are still outstanding. Next UX-05 is visual identity owner review, not an invented tool-mediated logo approval.

## DPT-04 V1 regression: duplicate assessment clicks, bounded waiting and late replies — 2026-10-23

**Source of continuity:** [PR #107](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/107) **MERGED** as main `4b40c0153147405c27310898c486bf5dd9030be4` after [Actions #39859932200](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39859932200) **4/4 PASS**; identical-word response identity, canceled request fail-closed, sanitized provider failures, mock single/blended correct consent. Older text below describing #107 as pending is historical, now superseded. All other DPT-01–03, DPT-05 and first DPT-04 tranches merged.

**Owner clarifies a previous V1 problem:** rapid repeated Speak submission and hanging/delayed feedback were practical issues in V1. Complete **one focused, zero-provider-cost V2 regression** without changing the language-scoring formula, curriculum or adding an AI feature:
- The existing `assessmentBusy` React state was not synchronous. Two `Check my pronunciation` activations in the same browser task could both pass the guard, with the second call aborting/replacing the first and causing unnecessary/possibly charged duplicate sends. Guard synchronously with `activeRequest.current` before any await, with no hidden retry. New connected browser test double-clicks in the same task, holds synthetic response, checks exactly one routed POST; Next discards late feedback, and a fresh subsequent take remains available.
- A provider/proxy stream could leave the optional Speak view stuck on `Checking pronunciation…`. Bound the complete client fetch **and JSON response-body consumption** at `45_000` ms using an internal AbortController and Promise.race. An upstream cancellation settles even when a test transport ignores AbortSignal; timeouts abort the fetch, report a clearly labelled user-safe failure, and NEVER automatically retry or assign score zero. The server-side 12/user and 30 total/day reserved allowance cannot be refunded once an upload started. This is a browser wait budget, not a guarantee the remote server stops computing or avoids incurred billing. Real phone/Safari/provider timing remains deferred.
- Fully mocked Vitest tests control fake time at 44,999/45,000 ms, hanging response and hanging body, uncooperative cancellation and no second fetch; desktop/mobile connected-browser tests exercise the actual Speak UI with a held synthetic provider response.
- Maintain opt-in and provider-off defaults; never send real microphone audio, connect Azure/Groq, save transcript/recording or assert phonetic correctness. The client cannot promise no provider charge if a request was already accepted; it does promise no deliberate second request from immediate repeat activation.

**Merge gate:** four final-head CI jobs PASS, then merge and record commit/CI in GitHub issue #2. No real device/acoustic sign-off or deployment. After this V1 regression, return to user-facing family learning/progression rather than extending backend robustness without evidence.

## DPT-04 transport cancellation and feedback identity gate — 2026-10-22

**Previously certified and merged:** DPT-04 first slice [PR #106](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/106) squash `7bef770cba08411bc22b5c2bef1f037e9ff6883b`, [CI #39857652966](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39857652966) **four of four PASS**; 10-second browser/server capture limits aligned and hermetic same-WAV Azure/Groq mocks, cross-user/request budgets, provider failures and explicit consent verified. This replaces earlier 'not yet merged' status below. DPT-03 #101–#104 and DPT-05 #105 are already merged with four green CI lanes each.

**This independent DPT-04 second slice is IN DEVELOPMENT, no merge until CI:** `requestSpeechFeedback` currently trusts any syntactically valid, 200-OK assessment response, without confirming `item_id` or engine matches the card and explicitly selected Azure or two-provider route. Under an accidentally swapped/stale JSON reply that could display the wrong word's aggregate or experimental blended score. An already-aborted request also reached `fetch`, and raw transport rejections could expose provider/proxy details in the UI's error string. Fix at the *client* trust boundary: pre-aborted take never starts fetch; post-request abortion must never return a grade; sanitize network/JSON exceptions; accept a result only when server `item_id` matches current source item and the successful engine matches selected mode; blended colour reference (or reviewed syllable reference) must match current source Polish exactly. Unsupported/malformed evidence fails closed without zero score, transcript, provider error/credential or hidden retry. Preserve existing `SpeakPanel key={current.id}` lifecycle/remount that cancels stale captures on item navigation.

**New hermetic Vitest transport tests:** explicitly enable feature via isolated module-mock/env test only (production opt-in remains unchanged); fully mock `fetch` (ZERO real Azure/Groq calls); assert aborted preflight and in-flight cancellation, no grade on swapped card/engine/reference, no fake zero on inconclusive, sanitized failed transport, HTTP 401/429/503, invalid JSON, exact original WAV bytes and explicit provider consent on single and blended routes, and truthful timing. Existing 14 blocked source references, no provider activation or billing, no XP/content/scoring calibration changes. Four final-head CI lanes PASS required before merging; this does not establish pronunciation scoring accuracy or physical microphone quality.

**DPT-04 first focused tranche (not merged until CI):** Audit existing Speech F0 and optional Whisper blend without calling providers. Findings: server WAV preflight enforces <=10.0 sec, while local `inspectPcm16` currently accepts up to 12 sec and can offer assessment for a take the service inevitably rejects. Align local maximum with server's 10 sec and add unit boundary test. Existing server already uses explicit email-verified auth, per-request 2-provider consent, distinct server-authoritative reference, exact source exclusion and a durable **12 verified requests per learner / 30 total per UTC day** ledger reserved *before* provider calls, not refunded on failure. Preserve those controls rather than introducing another XP/cost economy. Add hermetic tests proving same WAV digest and length delivered to the Azure and Groq adapters, once per provider, no provider invocation or usage recorded for clipped/silent/truncated/wrong/overlong input, global caps cannot be bypassed by another user or server restart, same shared cap applies to single and blended endpoints, canceled/network/provider failures remain `inconclusive` without grades or leaked SDK error, and Groq adapter makes a single timeout-bounded request with the exact WAV bytes and no implicit retry. Only in-memory synthetic mock fixture hashes; never store a user's voice/audio/hash, raw transcripts or keys. Existing 14 blocked references remain excluded.

**Gate:** Four CI lanes at final PR head, original fixed-score Whisper/Azure blend remains experimental and uncalibrated (the earlier 50 + 84 / 2 = 67 case is only a fixture). Source of truth: [v2/DEFERRED_PHYSICAL_TEST_ROADMAP.md](v2/DEFERRED_PHYSICAL_TEST_ROADMAP.md). **Prior merged and certified:** PR #98 `fb5a34f433c94f289353500df12614b3e84d8dc3` ([CI #39819216754 4/4 PASS](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39819216754)), PR #99 `8551cf6ff2c81a240f34eb7496fb253ffe99f70e` ([CI #39823136038 4/4 PASS](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39823136038)). But rapid repeated activation of one rendered answer could independently increment **recall and review attempts**, even when XP dedup kept the score constant. Add a short-lived, synchronous **per-current-step completion guard** to Learn/Read/Listen and Speak Next. It resets when the learner moves to another card/mode/session, so intentional separate retry remains allowed. The user should still earn normal XP and progress when offline.

Negative backup tests: syntactically invalid JSON, unsupported version, forged review schedule, invalid region and duplicate XP event IDs all leave six localStorage records unchanged even after a reload; success message forbidden. Unit test completion guard idempotency and legitimate repeat path.

**Gate:** Four existing GitHub CI lanes must pass at final head before merging. Real device, native audio, spoken phoneme review and production deploy remain deferred. Once green, proceed with DPT-03 synthetic cross-mode accessibility, layout and audio lifecycle audits.

## DPT-01 completed, DPT-02 storage/offline fault tranche (2026-10-22)

**DPT-01 merged:** PR #97 squash `9ec95e772974ff1b9829f211f746f52a2a7ef0bb` after [final-head CI #39815402210](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39815402210) passed all four lanes. **DPT-02 first coherent safety slice — in progress until green/merged:** audited `App.tsx` full backup restore and `scripts/sw-template.js` staged offline audio download. Formerly, restore mutated React practice/XP/lessons before asynchronous best-effort localStorage effects; a storage quota denial could still present "Backup restored" and falsely show progress that vanishes on reload. New `persistRestoredBackup` synchronously snapshots all six existing V2 local keys, writes validated personal lessons/progress/reviews/reading positions/expedition XP/last lesson, and rolls back all touched keys on any storage failure. Only after successful commit may React states and success text update. No local backup becomes account-synced family data. It remains a browser-local operation, not a fully crash-proof multi-key storage transaction.

Offline audio downloads already staged MP3s but could partially promote them into the durable cache before `QuotaExceededError`. New worker rollback retains originals and deletes promoted new files, reports an error instead of claiming full lesson readiness; normal STATUS continues to validate every required MP3. Cache rollback failure is explicit rather than a false green. Old cached files and app shell remain unaffected; no source audio changes.

Evidence includes Vitest round-trip + quota rollback tests, isolated real worker handler multi-file cache promotion failure + retry test, Playwright browser restore fault injection/reload/retry, and all preexisting desktop/mobile offline disconnect/reload tests. Four final-head CI lanes required for merge. Subsequent DPT-02 slices may cover failed REMOVE and explicit status/retry, storage crash recovery, and reconnect duplication as independently tested tasks. This tranche is **NOT YET MERGED** until final CI.

## Library stage navigation after 26-set copy audit (2026-10-21)

Owner asked to continue after [PR #93](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/93) **MERGED**, four CI lanes green and [issue #2](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/issues/2) updated. All original source and progress IDs unchanged; Numbers 1–20 is still one lesson with optional first ten/second ten.

**Next actual UX increment:** make the newly grouped Library genuinely navigable on mobile and desktop without scrolling through all 26 sets. A compact **Browse learning stage** selector appears above global search and legacy Sounds/Vocabulary/Reading filters. It offers **All stages (default), Fundamentals, Basic, Learning, Practice tools**, and **Your lessons only if the user has imported/created personal sets**; counts derive from current original catalog, never hardcoded, and do not reflect XP, certification or locked prerequisites. Stage browsing filters *presentation only*; selecting a stage resets any previous query and lesson-kind filter to prevent hidden, ambiguous result states. Conversely, entering search text or switching the kind filter resets stage browsing to **All stages**, so source filename search and Polish phrase search continue globally. An explicit All stages option always restores the 26-set catalog, and a no-matches state allows resetting. Stage selection is local UI state; it is not synced or persisted as learner progress. No new lessons, splitting, metadata, audio, stage unlocks, rewards, ASR calls or deploys. `v2/frontend/src/library-curriculum.ts` contains pure stage membership/count helpers, `App.tsx` wires accessibility labels/pressed state, and responsive CSS and browser+unit regressions cover counts, back-and-forth selection, original filename search and filters. Complete 4-lane CI and merge before declaring shipped.



## Library curriculum audit and human-readable set titles/descriptions (2026-10-21)

**Owner request:** "audit our library and update the title / description of each learning set. Also, lets explore a better organization. starting fundamental (sounds -> single words basic -> single words expand vocabulary) -> basic (multi-words -> short sentences) -> Learning (short conversations & reading)". This is a **curriculum/UX classification**, not a rewrite, new lessons, XP tier upgrade, phonetic/audio review, content split or prerequisite lock.

The reviewed source-of-truth manifest `v2/content/library-catalog.json` gives **all 26 original built-in sets** an explicit **learner title + accurate description + stage/track/order**. Full before/after inventory and caveats in `v2/content/LIBRARY_CURRICULUM_AUDIT.md`. Stages are:
- **Fundamentals (12):** 5 existing sound contrast exercises → Numbers 1–20 as the single familiar-number lesson → 6 existing expanded word/place/name/number collections. For example `Numbers_20-100` is renamed **Numbers Beyond 20** because the original source reaches 1,020; `Districts_of_Warszawa` now clearly notes non-district landmarks. No forced 8-item chunking; original Numbers 1–20 remains 1 lesson with optional 1–10 and 11–20 practice groups.
- **Basic (7):** `Conversation_1` greeting/intro **everyday expressions**, followed by six practical **useful phrases and short sentences** (shopping, restaurant ordering, dining preferences, directions, public transit, health/emergency requests).
- **Learning (6):** the 6 existing original reading modules in authored reading-path order (beginning with greeting exchanges and a grocery purchase); the prior 3 short conversations / listening and turn-taking exercises live inside two of these originals.
- **Practice tools (1):** existing `speak_swietokrzyska` isolated diagnostic, no trusted MP3. This is explicitly **outside the three-stage learner curriculum**; keep available without presenting it as a normal ready-to-learn foundation. Imported/personal lessons have a separate `Your lessons` section.

V2 content builder `attachLibraryCatalog` checks all 26 fixed built-in IDs have unique metadata and correct lesson-kind/stage/track mapping, title/description and position. Existing item IDs/Polish text/English text/original MP3s/reference blocks, 26 lessons and 482 items remain identical. V2 typed schema validates metadata and `App.tsx` displays grouped stage/track headings, honest descriptions on each tile and **old filename-compatible search**, while retaining normal Sound/Vocabulary/Reading filters and unrestricted direct lesson opening. The editorial filename ID is never renamed; progress, audio URLs, XP, family activity, backups, review queues and reading positions remain unchanged. UI is compact without arbitrary new badges/explainer boxes. Unit, migration, a11y and desktop/mobile browse checks cover stage counts, order, searchable original ID/title/Polish, accurate copy and intact old progress. **Update PR CI/merge status after verification**.



## E2 optional contextual Polish turn-taking in the same 3 micro-dialogues (2026-10-21)

Owner: "please continue" after merged PR #91, whose source-grounded E2 comprehension is complete. **New work** advances actual conversational response selection instead of creating more lessons, speculative audio or scoring. Add **Take a turn** as the third practice style beside Read dialogue and Listen first, inside the existing 3 dialogue panels of the 2 existing Read lessons. On opening Take a turn, show the **actual penultimate speaker's Polish prompt** and a Play control for its original mapped MP3; the learner selects an appropriate Polish reply from **three exact, original, audio-backed same-lesson reading lines**. Correct response is obligatorily the **next recorded dialogue turn**; two wrong choices are other exact original lesson phrases, not fabricated/written alternatives, not existing dialogue utterances, and are strictly checked against original IDs/text/audio. Authored relative turn positions, original candidate indices/text, and answer index reside durably in `v2/content/micro-dialogues.json` as `replyPractice`. Examples: responding `Dziękuję, mam się dobrze.` to `Cześć! Jak się masz?`; `Mam na imię Andrew.` to `Cześć, jak masz na imię?`; choosing `Poproszę czerwone jabłka.` after the shop assistant's red-vs-green question. The name `Andrew` and other source lines are **original imported reading content**, not personalized replacements.

After a selection the UI identifies the appropriate response, shows its original English translation, permits replaying the original mapped reference clip, and provides a retry control. Switching Read/Listen/Take a turn resets the temporary answer only. These are **textual pragmatic choices, not microphone or phonetic scores**; no new XP, no saved accuracy metrics, no new lessons, source audio, continuous synthesized dialogue, AI inference, Azure/Groq requests, recording upload or deployment. Existing Read/Listen first and prior E2 comprehension remain intact. The strict migration and V2 schema reject missing/duplicate choices, wrong correct answer, unsupported source items, wrong turn links and missing/flagged MP3 references; desktop/mobile browser and unit tests verify all 3. Original 26 lessons / 482 items / 6 Read / 51 passages / 24 authored reading checks / 14 practice-only Speak blocks remain. Original MP3 native-listening authenticity gate is still pending. **PR/CI/merge state recorded once confirmed.**



## E2 short-dialogue comprehension added to original three scenes (2026-10-21)

After [PR #90](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/90) delivered three source-matched 2–3-turn exchanges inside the original `Basic_01R` and `Grocery_store_very_basic_shopping` **reading lessons**, the next substantive E2 increment adds **one authored meaning/intent comprehension question per scene**, not new words, new dialogue modules, phonetic grading or more invented recordings. Questions reference only exact original dialogue: (1) how Person B answers `Cześć! Jak się masz?` (they are well), (2) what Person B reveals in `Mam na imię Andrew.` (their name), (3) which apples are chosen in `Poproszę czerwone jabłka.` (red). Three individually curated three-choice answer sets with explicit correct index and short explanation live in `v2/content/micro-dialogues.json`, build-time validated for completeness, unique options and valid answer; the normal strict reading lesson schema additionally validates the rendered data.

**Interaction:** Inside the existing optional micro-dialogue panel, "Check understanding" allows a single answer and shows the correct/wrong explanation. **Listen first** initially hides both the Polish transcript and English translations, so learners may use the existing per-turn Play controls and answer before reveal. Answering does not automatically reveal Polish, while learners can still explicitly reveal it; translation may be shown after answering or revealing. "Read dialogue" displays full source Polish; switching Read/Listen practice style resets only the **transient** answer, not user progress. This interaction has **zero XP, no permanent quiz record, no extra lessons, no AI or provider requests**; individual MP3s retain their unverified native-listening gate and approved 0.75/0.85/1 speeds. Original catalog remains 26 modules, 482 items, 6 reading lessons, 51 reading items, 24 passage comprehension checks, 14 practice-only Speak blockers. This is a small pedagogical comprehension step; any future speak-back or continuous-dialogue playback requires separate UX/audio acceptance. **PR/CI merge status must be recorded after completion.**



## E2 pilot — 3 actual micro-dialogues within existing Read lessons, no new lessons (2026-10-21)

Continue after PR #89's **14 practice-only Speak guard** and owner acceptance of natural pedagogy (Numbers 1–20 is ONE lesson; 1–10/11–20 optional sections; never arbitrary lesson splits). The next E2 goal is **short, 2–3-turn conversations in Read and Listen**, accessible without introducing new lesson IDs, hypothetical speaker audio, false graded Speak, or artificial XP. Source content and individually mapped MP3s are retained in the original 6 reading modules. A small pilot uses **three curated scenes** in `v2/content/micro-dialogues.json`:

- `Basic_01R` lines 0–1, Person A/B: **How are you?** (`Cześć! Jak się masz?` / `Dziękuję, mam się dobrze.`).
- `Basic_01R` lines 2–3, Person A/B: **What's your name?** (`Cześć, jak masz na imię?` / `Mam na imię Andrew.`). The name is from ORIGINAL historical source text and audio index; do not silently replace it or claim it reflects a new recording.
- `Grocery_store_very_basic_shopping` lines 1–3, Customer / Shop assistant / Customer: **Buying apples** (request a kilo of apples, choose red/green, answer red). Each exact original Polish line and MP3 stays unchanged.

These are **optional conversation practices inside the TWO existing reading lessons**, not three new curriculum modules. `attachMicroDialogues()` fails closed against source drift, missing/unmapped individual MP3s, duplicate/unordered lines, unsafe flagged audio, wrong speaker sequence or missing metadata. Generated reading lessons gain `microDialogues` metadata containing **original item IDs only**; the existing Read and Listen modes, 24 reading questions, six reading modules, 51 reading passages, 26 modules/482 items, original per-item progress and XP rules remain unchanged. In Read lesson mode, compact buttons open a separate **Short conversation** panel, with original full Polish two-speaker script, per-turn play (no fake merged audio), `Listen first` hiding Polish until explicit reveal, an English translation reveal, and only approved **0.75×/0.85×/1×** playback speeds. This panel neither persists attempts nor awards XP. It explicitly uses original recordings but does NOT claim native-speaker or per-voice approval: audio content/provenance still requires listening verification. On exiting the optional practice, the saved reading position/record stays untouched. Desktop/mobile browser and source validation tests required. No Azure/Groq/TTS synthesis, upload, billing, or score route changes. GitHub CI/PR merge outcome to be recorded after validation.

## OWNER DECISION — Single-pass Azure scoring; drop manual/forced-alignment work from release path (2026-10-20)

**Latest instruction, superseding the R&D path after PR #67:** pronunciation must be **simple and fast**: listen to Polish example → speak into mic → **one** Azure `pl-PL` scripted pronunciation assessment of the original recording against the known target → show overall pronunciation accuracy and **syllable ratings only if genuinely reliable and automatically derived** → repeat or Next; optional existing sound-practice link remains. **No learner manual waveform boundary marking, clip cutting, MFA forced-alignment model, Whisper→TTS→Azure, multi-model assessment cascade, hidden extra inference or slow review steps** in production or as launch prerequisites. Research-only files from PR #67 can stay archived/available but do not expand or integrate them into the learning flow. Whisper is an optional *separate* recognition modality, not the pronunciation grader.

**Verified provider fact (Microsoft Learn, checked 2026-10-20):** Azure supports Polish `pl-PL` scripted pronunciation assessment with a single call, returning aggregate/word scores and optionally ordered phoneme-level numeric scores. Its native **syllable groups and named phonemes are not supported for Polish** (syllable grouping currently `en-US` only). Source: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/how-to-pronunciation-assessment . Do not suggest Azure directly returns per-syllable Polish percentages. A precomputed, expert-checked target phoneme-to-syllable grouping may possibly allow a *fast, deterministic, in-memory* mapping of existing Azure ordered phoneme numeric results with **zero second model calls**. This is a feasibility experiment, NOT a proven algorithm or authentic grading result. Verify *actual raw adult Polish provider response shape* and exact phone count/sequence first; do not assign labels to unnamed phones or average arbitrary indexes when any count, alignment, silence/omission, pronunciation or confidence check fails. Validate against a few native and learner recordings. If not credible, stop—publish the word-level Azure estimate with existing pronunciation-guide and optional Sound Lab links, show no syllable percentages. **No zero scores invented; no fake Whisper 50/50/0/100.** Never hold up useful word-level practice or add a manual fallback.

**Implementation priority:** avoid more infrastructure and research tooling. Existing `v2/server/speech_assessment.py` already invokes one Azure SDK call with phoneme granularity and intentionally returns only real uncalibrated aggregate values; existing `SpeakPanel` and `assessment.ts` already support a guarded future validated syllable UI. Next substantive action is the smallest **one-word, one-response evidence check** using *already-saved approved private Azure raw result* (if available) or an explicit no-cost/consented research request, then decide yes/no for a deterministic, pre-reviewed grouping. Only if it passes the guard should implementation emit `reviewed-acoustic-syllables-v1`. Keep voice upload opt-in, free-tier quota, account privacy and existing no-additional-cost requirement. The privately opened syllable WAV lab is diagnostic-only and **not** part of the planned user experience. Do not build syllable practice merely to score the sound; Sound Lab cluster exercises remain optional regular lessons.

The historical Whisper 50/50/0/100 was an ASR-text proxy, **never a real syllable-acoustic grade**. Do not automatically align raw Polish unnamed-phoneme scores to syllables, split aggregate score, fake grade or suggest specific mistakes until actual phonetic calibration validates a mapping. Sound Lab manual KRZY link remains optional and not a diagnosed weak sound.

**Next useful physical evidence**: privately inspect already saved adult Azure **raw** Polish result if present (old sanitized latency file insufficient); get the small structural counts; if enough reliable phoneme evidence exists, human-review phoneme-to-syllable alignment using explicit native controls and learner labels. Obtain licensed/human-reviewed exact-word Świętokrzyska reference (existing `ulica Świętokrzyska` longer recording is not the exact standalone target). Only then implement the true syllable scorer on backend behind opt-in and secure family account gates. Existing Render Free V2 deployment stays account/assessment disabled; code UI may deploy but no real syllable numbers are shown. See [V2 pronunciation contract](v2/PRONUNCIATION_PRACTICE_UX.md).

## Speak pronunciation score → repeat + optional Sound Lab detour — OWNER-APPROVED UX (2026-10-20)

**New decisive owner correction:** "I dont want to go to a suggested correction, getting a realistic score is enough for now." Core loop: **listen to authentic Polish word → record/replay own attempt → receive credible per-syllable scores on the same Polish word → record again until personally satisfied OR tap Next**. No scripted articulation corrections, tutoring wall of text, forced pass threshold, score-dependent navigation, extra assessment stages or mandatory exercises. Approved optional detour: if a reliably scored weak sound maps to an **existing, well-authored sound-practice set**, show discreet `Practise krzy sounds →` / `Practise cz →` link; enter ordinary Speak-capable targeted Sound Lab practice, with obvious **Back to [origin word]** that restores exact source lesson, item, mode and guide state, then retry original word. Do not reset original lesson, lose place, or double-credit XP. The pronunciation guide remains **OFF by default**, English syllable cues appear only when ON, and scoring remains visible after assessment independently of guide state. The full exact feature, data, scoring-confidence/consent, navigation, sound-mapping and acceptance contract is now at **[`v2/PRONUNCIATION_PRACTICE_UX.md`](v2/PRONUNCIATION_PRACTICE_UX.md)** (committed to main `24b5332c8ab0a21f6155f7e51c26db38de9159a0`), and supersedes any "suggested correction" feature proposal.

**Grounded code audit:** existing `SpeakPanel.tsx` captures/replays and offers Repeat/Next but only displays Azure aggregate Accuracy/Fluency/Completeness when a provider is available; no live per-syllable scores. Existing `phonetic_similarity.py` does calculate the old 50/50/0/100 from **Whisper written hypotheses**, NOT true per-syllable acoustic measurement; never grade learners or derive sound weaknesses from that uncalibrated proxy. Curated four-syllable English guide exists, still needs Polish speaker review. Built-in Świętokrzyska test item has **no authentic reference MP3**, must satisfy listen-first requirement; Sound Lab has `c/cz/ć`, `s/sz/ś`, `z/ż/ź`, `l/ł` comparison sets, NOT scored targeted sound practice or return navigation. `krzy` (~ /kʂɨ/, rz devoiced after k) is NOT the same as `cz` (/t͡ʂ/): do not connect misleading exercises. Existing React `openLesson` resets current index/mode/guide so exact return-context support needed before adding optional links. No new fake scores, sound-practice links, UI modifications, provider uploads, new paid resources or external voice calls were made in this design-tranche documentation. **Next work**: validate measured per-syllable acoustic evidence and reference audio, then implement the minimal feedback loop; map optional links only where matching sound practice content exists and allow exact resume.

## PR #56 MERGED — first syllable-colour phonetic similarity score and private review (2026-10-19)

**[PR #56](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/56) MERGED**, squash `53b389bbedc6c9fca0e82c5e2cec3ce6c69c69b3`, after **all four CI lanes PASS** on exact head `6e687d01b5f8d808730c57d523a19dfa30d87c67`: [CI #39292933984](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39292933984). No Azure/Groq requests or real user voice in CI. **Explicit owner request** after completing all physical Azure + Whisper trials: "start with the phonetic similarity score" and simplify pronunciation feedback to ONE highlighted Polish word with **per-syllable colour/score**, tap a syllable to show EXACT *hypothesized* difference between said vs target sounds, identify problematic Polish letter combinations. Owner confirms Whisper's `Sweczo Seska` / `Sveczo Seska` were close to what they really spoke, and highlights W/V spellings that sound equivalent: DO NOT penalize purely orthographic W vs V or suggest Whisper output is just meaningless because ASR word match failed. This is a positive **development plan extension**, not unrelated QA sliver; prioritize a useful learner surface over aggregate score-dominated UX. Simpler main UI: syllables colour-coded, single overall estimated similarity, tap for inferred sound-unit substitutions/additions/omissions and Polish letter group being practised. No compulsory speech grade, no XP gates, no manual file input requirement in final learner experience.

**Technical honesty critical:** Whisper emits WRITTEN WORDS, not literal spoken phonemes; per-syllable unit differences derived from spelling are **hypotheses** and CANNOT be asserted as the exact sounds a person articulated. We must not turn WER into an uncalibrated "phoneme score" masquerading as verified acoustic measurement. Prior Azure 84/100/100 is provider raw/unvalidated. The first v1 metric therefore explicitly called **`UNVALIDATED_TRANSCRIPT_DERIVED_SOUND_UNIT_SIMILARITY`**; never a certified Polish pronunciation grade. Score = 100 * max(0,1 - weighted edit cost / target sound-unit count), syllable-level and overall, exploratory weights and precise versioned contract. Compare BOTH Whisper model outputs, W/V normalized equivalent, use common Polish digraph units (e.g. `rz` after k devoices), flag model disagreements NEUTRAL grey rather than falsely confident red, and show guessed units as such. Initial hard-verified syllable segmentation only for `Świę|to|krzy|ska`; fail-closed for other terms rather than pretend comprehensive Polish syllabification.

**Consolidated initial development tranche**:
- New `v2/benchmarks/phonetic_similarity.py`: **100% OFFLINE**, reads already-completed private original WAV and existing `azure-compare-01.jsonl` + `whisper-01.jsonl` via the existing SHA/reference verified paired report. NO Azure/Groq calls, no token/file modifications. Deterministic weighted Polish proxy sound-unit alignment, syllable-scored statuses `close/review/focus/uncertain`, per-syllable edit details and both model hypotheses.
- Generates an owner-local, mode-0600, Git-ignored, completely standalone `phonetic-review-01.html` with mobile-sized syllable segments, coloured/highlighted target, clickable/keyboard `details` to show what the output may have heard, expected units and specific letter groups. No external scripts, analytics, audio embed or remote assets. Colour is supplemented by text and scores for accessibility.
- `v2/benchmarks/test_phonetic_similarity.py` mock/unit-only covers W/V equivalent, exact spelling 100 (explicitly NOT accurate sound proof), actual two incorrect Groq spellings, strong `-ska` match, instability when model results differ, fail-closed unknown targets/invalid data, escaping of HTML, exclusive private file, and NO mutations to original saved evidence.
- `v2/benchmarks/PHONETIC_SIMILARITY.md` documents zero-provider owner preview with `python v2/benchmarks/phonetic_similarity.py --wav "$WAV" --azure-result ... --whisper-result ... --html-output PathToPolish-Private-AI/swietokrzyska-01/phonetic-review-01.html` then `open` locally; NEVER re-run Groq/Azure, overwrite previous outputs, upload voice or track private transcripts. The real owner will need `git pull` and run the offline script to SEE estimated scores; none have yet been computed on owner actual files in CI.

**Release boundary and next planned integration**: This is a **research preview and scoring/UI contract**, not yet wired to every live Speak page. The existing V2 Speak UI provides opt-in Azure aggregate assessment only; it has NO live Groq phoneme stream and cannot honestly display verified syllable errors today. After physical review of private HTML and score credibility, only THEN connect this same minimal component into a normal Speak lesson automatically without manually typing/importing. ## PR #55 MERGED — OWNER real Azure-vs-Whisper same-WAV result and WER correction (2026-10-19)

**[PR #55](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/pull/55) MERGED**, squash `495a1f0097b8ea6fdbf9eb724e5d86a326aea6cb` after **all four CI lanes PASS** on exact PR head `3fd8b1162b9a7179341625ef7fd173be0aae9135`: [CI #39269242693](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/actions/runs/39269242693). CI evaluated the WER correction offline only; it did not reproduce real Groq or Azure calls. **Actual owner Mac, no simulated/provider mock:** Original consenting adult V2 Speak `Świętokrzyska` 3.12s private 16kHz PCM16 WAV SHA256 `0f4ff4b3d01165dc5e27fd9c10336f19aedf4aefdca430798c2b1777e5df1626`. Owner installed permanent Groq local key `PathToPolish-Private-AI/.env.groq` and read-only Groq model-list access check **PASSED**, `whisper-large-v3` and `whisper-large-v3-turbo` listed, 0 audio on preflight. Then owner explicitly approved and performed **TWO ACTUAL Groq transcription calls**, 0 new Azure, no automatic retries. Owner private Groq result `PathToPolish-Private-AI/swietokrzyska-01/whisper-01.jsonl` **COMPLETE; preserve DO NOT rerun or overwrite**. Original private Azure result `azure-compare-01.jsonl` also COMPLETE; preserve. These files are ON OWNER MAC ONLY, do NOT commit voice/keys/private JSONL.

| Tested provider | Observed elapsed | Actual result |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Azure ordinary recognize_once Python SDK | 2459.7ms | raw Accuracy 84.0 / Fluency 100.0 / Completeness 100.0 |
| Azure explicit preconnect (confirmed event), SDK total INCLUDING 423.9ms preconnect | 1923.3ms | same raw 84 / 100 / 100 |
| Groq Whisper large-v3 (curl HTTP) | **1034.9ms** | literal transcript **`Sweczo Seska`**, NOT `Świętokrzyska` |
| Groq Whisper large-v3-turbo (curl HTTP) | **470.8ms** | literal transcript **`Sveczo Seska`**, NOT `Świętokrzyska` |

**Critical interpretation:** Both Whisper models produced incorrect TWO-token transcripts for ONE reference word. `exact_word_match=false` for both; both WER **2.0** (two word-level edits: substitution + insertion per single-word denominator). This is valid **200% word error rate**, not impossible: single-reference-word WER can exceed 1.0 when ASR inserts extra words. Previous code docstring/runbook/workflow claimed WER only 0 or 1; **INCORRECT, now being fixed explicitly** and unit-locked. A transcript mismatch could reflect speaker performance, unfamiliar Polish place-name/language-model bias, or other recognition effects, cannot attribute cause or infer actual pronunciation. Raw Azure 84/100/100 not human-calibrated or proof of correct sound articulation. Whisper has NO measured phoneme accuracy or credible pronunciation score. Turbo single-shot HTTP time ~4.1x faster than preconnected Azure SDK total but fundamentally DIFFERENT performance instrumentation, sequential timings and n=1; do NOT claim production p95 or speed certainty. Previous ~20.4s wrong-target Azure tail remains unresolved. Both Whisper variants FAILED exact recognition of this Polish word on user recording; replacing Azure pronunciation feedback with Whisper-only strict text match would be bad learner UX and could unfairly penalize learners. Do not enforce incorrect transcription as XP progression gate.

**Product recommendation now:** Preserve Azure as provisional provider for detailed Polish pronunciation assessment pending real native and adult human-labeled tests; treat Whisper Turbo as fast-but-not-demonstrated-correct recognition component, NOT sufficient for graded practice. Future improvement should explicitly extend approved study plan to (1) an independently verified natural native Polish `Świętokrzyska` recording (not invented), (2) controlled consenting adult learner recordings/human qualitative phoneme annotations, (3) end-to-end browser/server latency monitoring and bounded user wait (target consistently <5s). Do not automatically schedule or run more paid-capable provider calls, and do not proliferate new tiny proof gates. The user has already invested a lot in tooling; address product usefulness next.

**Code/documentation:** `v2/benchmarks/swietokrzyska_whisper.py` docstring and interpretation corrected for WER >1; `test_swietokrzyska_whisper.py` adds unit regression for actual two-token hypotheses; `v2/benchmarks/SWIETOKRZYSKA_LATENCY.md` records exact physical real numbers, key installed, evidence paths and no-repeat requirement. No changes to original SHA-locked WAV, raw Azure scores or privately saved outputs. Before merge, CI is synthetic only; DO NOT suggest physical rerun.

## OWNER REAL AZURE LATENCY SAMPLE COMPLETE — four correct-reference Speech SDK calls (2026-10-19)

**Owner executed the merged PR #48 `v2/benchmarks/azure_short_latency.py --count 4 --send` locally on Mac. This is real provider evidence, NOT CI mocks, and the owner explicitly confirmed the Azure F0/public-audio/no-paid flags.** Actual SHA-locked original public WAVs, alternating 1.250-second *Dzień dobry* and 1.579-second *Dobry wieczór* against their CORRECT Azure references. Exactly FOUR Azure requests, ZERO Groq, FOUR scored successes, all raw uncalibrated Accuracy/Fluency/Completeness **100/100/100**, no canceled/unscored attempts or SDK diagnostic enums. Response SDK monotonic wall-clock durations by attempt: **4052.9 ms** (`greeting-day`), **6666.4 ms** (`greeting-evening`), **4019.6 ms** (`greeting-day`), **1470.0 ms** (`greeting-evening`). **Median 4036.2 ms, worst 6666.4 ms; 1/4 >=5s, zero >=10s, zero >=20s**. The 20,411.3-ms prior wrong-reference control DID NOT recur in this small four-request sample; cannot estimate actual production p95 or rule out severe tail latency. User explicitly says **20 seconds is a deal-breaker**; four-second typical response also not the eventual desired learning UX. Original private evidence stored at `PathToPolish-Private-AI/public-polish-live-01/azure-latency-four-01.jsonl` (DO NOT OVERWRITE) and old `azure-wrong-reference-01.jsonl`, `azure-only-diagnostic-01.jsonl`, `results-01.jsonl` preserved.

**Engineering finding:** Both `v2/benchmarks/azure_pilot.py::azure_scripted_assess` and current `v2/server/speech_assessment.py::azure_scripted_assess` create NEW `SpeechConfig`, `AudioConfig`, `SpeechRecognizer` and then call blocking `recognize_once()` on every request. Benchmarks time the whole call but cannot isolate SDK setup, connection/TLS, file transfer, endpoint silence detection, inference and transport. Repetition on same WAV with variable 6.67→1.47s does not prove any root cause. A previous `CFNetwork SSLHandshake failed (-9806)` warning appeared on the 20.4s run, not observed in the owner's four-call timing paste; no causal attribution established. Microsoft documents that `Connection.from_recognizer(...).open(False)` may initiate preconnection, and `Connection.open()` return does NOT guarantee ready; need connected events and actual measurement before treating preconnection as optimization. Microsoft also documents silence segmentation tradeoffs; DO NOT blindly lower timeout or cut Polish slow learner speech.
Sources:
- https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/python/api/azure-cognitiveservices-speech/azure.cognitiveservices.speech.connection
- https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/how-to-control-connections
- https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/how-to-recognize-speech

**Decision:** Do NOT replace Azure based on a single prior 20-second result, but DO NOT declare the latency problem solved from four runs. Keep pronunciation optional/ungraded and record/replay accessible. Next performance-oriented development increment should instrument full **app/HTTP/SDK timings** using sanitized metrics, test whether an SDK connection lifecycle optimization (pre-open and/or reuse, with privacy/isolation and no hidden extra billable calls) actually reduces cold-per-request delay, and assess a sensible UX wait/failure boundary without fabricated scores or unapproved automatic retries. Avoid repeated synthetic provider calls just to create certification work; consider an A/B only if/when a concrete code optimization is ready and F0 quota is reapproved. Family game/XP must NOT gate on uncalibrated Azure results. Owner's result detailed in `v2/benchmarks/AZURE_SHORT_LATENCY_RUN.md`.

New result against deliberately wrong reference `Dobry wieczór`: **AccuracyScore 50.0, FluencyScore 100.0, CompletenessScore 50.0**; paired positive-minus-negative **50.0, 0.0, 50.0**. Returned `status=WRONG_TARGET_AZURE_CONTROL_COMPLETED`, `network_requests_performed=1`, `groq_requests_performed=0`, `azure_latency_ms=20411.3`, `wrong_target_result=returned_scores`, `same_audio_confirmed=true`, `grading_verified=false`, `phoneme_diagnosis_verified=false`; saved privately at `PathToPolish-Private-AI/public-polish-live-01/azure-wrong-reference-01.jsonl` (EXISTS, NEVER overwrite). **Interpretation:** Azure distinguished the correct entire phrase from a WRONG two-word target while keeping its fluency rating high. Microsoft's scripted PA `CompletenessScore` measures share of reference words and `FluencyScore` concerns pauses/flow, so this pattern is coherent; phrases both contain `dobry`, but the precise 50.0 values must NOT be attributed to one common word without inspecting word-level evidence. One native paired control CANNOT demonstrate reliable Polish phoneme-specific error detection, calibration, child scoring, or a production cutoff. Keep Speak basic workflow unchanged (listen → record → replay → optional feedback → retry/next), no mandatory uploads or XP tied to unvalidated Azure scores. Keep provisional Azure-for-pronunciation and Whisper Turbo-for-transcript roles subject to real adult data.

**Reliability observation:** The owner saw `CFNetwork SSLHandshake failed (-9806)` during this request and **20,411.3ms** elapsed before scoring completed. Apple's `errSSLClosedAbort` means a TLS connection closed due to an error; no direct proof this caused the slow response or that the successful SDK result is invalid. Track as a potential connection/latency UX issue; do not chase a speculative network root cause or run repeated paid inference without reason.

**Next real VALUE tranche, not infrastructure:** sample a small voluntarily recorded ADULT set using the EXISTING Speak record/replay/private WAV export. Use target words/phrases that test Polish sounds `s/ś`, `c/cz`, `sz/ś`; each correct and intentionally wrong attempt for the SAME reference, with independent Polish-proficient human listeners labeling target correctness and what was actually heard BEFORE reading Azure responses. ## Read first in every new development session
1. [V2_REBUILD_PLAN.md](V2_REBUILD_PLAN.md) — authoritative product scope, architecture direction, priorities, delivery tranches, acceptance criteria.
2. [Issue #2 — V2 rebuild umbrella](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/issues/2) — execution checklist and checkpoint statuses.
3. [Issue #1 — Polish pronunciation benchmark](https://github.com/AndrewDionne/LearnPolish/issues/1) — tests and free-first provider policies.
4. Latest V2 PRs, commits and CI evidence before choosing a next task.

## Owner's approved decisions
- **Keep the `AndrewDionne/LearnPolish` repository.** Rebuild clean V2 inside it, then replace the unused legacy app. Do not create a new repository unless later evidence justifies it.
- Prioritize an actually functioning **Learn, Speak, Read, Listen** application, not incremental patches to old generated pages.
- **Pronunciation is a core feature**, not just word recognition. **Test Whisper large-v3 first** against a clean Azure `pl-PL` pronunciation baseline, on identical recorded audio. Grade pronunciation only from sound-level evidence; do not infer it from transcription alone.
- Prefer **recurring free AI API allowances / local open-source execution** during research. Verify quotas in the real account. Never incur paid API usage or auto-top-ups without explicit approval.
- User lessons are user data; **never generate entire HTML pages and Git commit/push** to create a learning set.
- Use coherent, larger-but-reviewable, end-to-end capability tranches; proportionate automated tests, synthetic browser/screenshot UX review before physical testing, and merge eligible verified work into `main`.
- GitHub is the durable source of continuity across chats. Update this file, the plan, and issues/PRs when a material decision changes.

## Continuation procedure
1. Fetch current GitHub status; do not infer implementation completion from old chat.
2. Check umbrella issue and code. Choose the next unfinished acceptance checkpoint, identifying dependencies and relevant tests.
3. Develop in a branch; execute available checks, report what is tested vs unverified.
4. Merge only when acceptance evidence and repository policy permit; keep issues/checklist and this workflow current.
5. In the user summary, state actual files/PRs/commit changes and next step, without claiming unrun tests or unbuilt feature## PHYSICAL FIRST THREE-ENGINE EXPERIMENT — SUCCESSFUL Groq, Azure INCONCLUSIVE (2026-10-18)

## Research design direction: slower speech + Azure pl-PL parallel baseline (2026-10-17)

**Owner exploration:** Slowing playback might improve Polish Whisper transcription because beginners speak slowly anyway; asked whether to bring Azure into research now. **Recommended hypothesis-driven design, NOT yet coded, sourced or tested:**

1. Distinguish learner-friendly **playback-speed control** (may use 0.75–1.0×) from **model input transformations** (never infer a pronunciation grade from processed audio). A genuinely slow-speaking learner is NOT acoustically equivalent to offline time-stretch of a native sample; slowed playback should remain an optional learner UI feature and offline diagnostic.
2. For new **human-verified, naturally spoken Polish sentence recordings** (not existing two too-short Commons words), A/B each immutable source at **1.00× original**, **0.85×**, and optionally **0.75×** pitch-preserving time stretch (FFmpeg `atempo=0.85` / `atempo=0.75`, https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg-filters.html#atempo). Preserve untouched 1× originals; give each derivative its own checksum/provenance/timing; model same reference; reject distorted/clipped/stretch artifacts on independent listening. Include **naturally slowly spoken** learner speech as separate original recordings at 1×. Do not multiply API costs until free-tier and user approval reverified; start local offline preparation.
3. **Bring Azure Speech Pronunciation Assessment `pl-PL` into parallel baseline now**, but distinguish roles: Groq Whisper Large-v3/Turbo = transcription and known-text recognition; Azure = scripted reference-based pronunciation **accuracy, fluency, completeness, word miscue and phoneme accuracy scores**. Microsoft docs confirm Polish locale among 33 PA locales, but **named expected phoneme output is supported only for en-US (IPA), en-US/zh-CN (SAPI); for Polish, scores can lack phoneme names; spoken phoneme candidates, syllable groups and prosody are en-US-only**. We must inspect and preserve real raw `pl-PL` JSON and test whether its scores are useful on native speaker vs rated adult learner Polish contrast errors rather than promising Polish phoneme explanations. Docs https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/language-support?tabs=pronunciation-assessment and https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/how-to-pronunciation-assessment#supported-features-per-locale .
4. **Do NOT feed stretched audio to Azure for a fair pronunciation-grade comparison.** Its fluency/speed-related signals can be confounded by artificial time processing. Use **unaltered 1× naturally recorded learner audio**, same SHA as recognition engines, with native listener labels; natural slower learner speech is in-scope. For exploratory transcription tempo tests, only Groq ASR variants get tempo derivatives, not phonetic grading.
5. **Costs:** Azure Speech F0 published 5 hours of free real-time STT per month (https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/pricing/details/speech/), but verify **actual user Azure resource SKU F0, feature entitlement, current use and no S0/paid consumption** before a single PA call. Implement isolated credential handling, raw response capture and small budget hard stop after free-tier confirmation. No Azure account, keys, resource or paid calls assumed; avoid any external voice uploads absent explicit informed consent.
6. Model-selection gate: test at least 8–12 intact 5–15s **natural sentence** controls with independently listened exact transcripts; 2+ voices where permitted; originals vs optional tempo transforms; then adult learner clips with independent ratings. Separate ASR WER from PA per-word/phoneme accuracy agreement and calibration. No more single-word Groq tests. No code/design commitments on artificial slowing until natural-corpus evidence.

**Never relocate** the user's `repo/PathToPolish-Private-AI/` folder or previously imported native files. Previous four Groq requests completed successfully but their word clips remain smoke tests only.

## First actual v3 vs Turbo results — strict-match bug identified; offline replay (2026-10-17)

**Owner's real four results** (same immutable SHA WAVs in unchanged `repo/PathToPolish-Private-AI/commons-native-01/`): `czapka` → v3 `Czap, czapka.`, Turbo `Czap-czapka.`; `ciasto` → v3 `Często.`, Turbo `CZASTO`. ## Reward-system direction — owner decision 2026-10-16 (design only)

- **Retire the Pierogi characters from the intended V2 reward UX.** They remain in historical code/assets for now; do not spend time ripping them out before replacing the view. Shift from Gold-as-currency toward **XP for real effort, additional XP for demonstrable improvement, and team achievements**. Reuse existing earned-event data where safe rather than discarding it.
- **Private family learning groups**, with encouraging, optional and friendly weekly leaderboards. Recognize distinct strengths: effort, most improvement relative to baseline, highest unlocked learning difficulty, consistency, and contribution to the group. Shared progress is permanent even when weekly leaderboards reset. No individual speech recordings exposed publicly.
- **Open interactive map of Poland:** any Polish region can be selected at any time, irrespective of adjacency or prior unlocks. Family members can coordinate organically, but the application **does not require votes or orchestrate a route**. Individuals can independently focus/contribute XP toward a different region ("go rogue"); their progress still benefits the family's map. Unlocked regions remain permanently open. Region costs grow with progression according to a moderate configurable power curve, not a mandated fixed route.
- **Learning difficulty and reward growth must rise together.** Later/harder lessons earn more qualifying XP, while more costly regions and anti-repetition eligibility make exploring all Poland via beginner-content farming impractical. Keep effort recognition meaningful to beginners; avoid punishing children or low-volume participants.
- **Design for functioning pronunciation scoring at launch**, rather than creating temporary scoring/reward code to remove later. Make the reward model accept *verified learning outcomes* from Learn/Read/Listen and a future vetted Speak phonetic assessor through the same small stable interface. Speaking improvement bonuses depend on measured change from a learner baseline. An absent/inconclusive result is not zero or a fake grade, and Speak still earns base effort XP for a usable attempt. Do not tie XP to Whisper transcription accuracy alone.
- **Avoid make-work / premature implementation.** This is a product design decision, NOT a request for an XP rewrite now. Keep existing PR #13/#14 behavior operational until one coherent UI + event-ledger + group/map tranche can replace it. Exact XP amounts, difficulty classification, region-cost formula, allocation rules and social access control need refinement during implementation; do not hard-code arbitrary constants at this stage. No content authoring. Continue the real AI evaluation with existing privacy and zero-spend gates.

## Priority pivot — owner-approved 2026-10-16

**Pause all new lesson/content authoring and content QA tranches. Work in this sequence:**
1. **Four learning modes E2E:** coherent Home → Library → Learn/Speak/Read/Listen → response/capture → meaningful completion → next mode, with audio reset, feedback, progress and real phone UX. Keep voice assessment **ungraded** until proven.
2. **Empirically test pronunciation AI:** Whisper large-v3 first, paired with corrected Azure native pl-PL results on **identical WAV bytes**. Private consented learner speech, labelled by human Polish judgement; prohibit paid use and auto-upgrades. A green synthetic benchmark harness does **not** mean the AI has been tested.
3. **Restore motivation/reward for work:** legacy Gold, 500-Gold weekly Path goal versus personal weekly goal, total Gold, daily streak, profile progress and eventually Pierogi character expressions/milestones. Reward completed, validated effort, not arbitrary pronunciation grades or clicking Skip. No coins with cash value.
